  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子局部可测试码（qLTC）在量子PCP猜想与量子纠错中具有重要意义，但此前在量子比特上同时实现常数码率、常数相对距离与常数soundness的显式构造长期是难点。

Method: 构造量子LDPC CSS码：量子比特上的CSS码，具有常数权重稳定子（LDPC），并配备常数权重、常数soundness的局部测试器，且为显式家族。

Result: 得到的qLTC同时满足：常数码率、常数相对距离、常数权重局部测试器与常数soundness，即在渐近意义上为“good”。

Conclusion: 首次（或显著推进）给出量子比特上渐进良好qLTC的显式构造，联通cLTC与qLTC的构造图景。

Abstract: We construct explicit families of asymptotically good quantum locally testable codes over qubits. More precseily, we construct explicit quantum LDPC CSS codes over qubits having constant rate, constant relative distance and constant weight local testers with constant soundness.

</details>


### [95] [Parallel quantum channel discrimination and numerical ranges in tensor product subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20781)
*Adam Bílek,Paulina Lewandowska,Ryszard Kukulski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究并行方案下的完美量子信道区分，提出结合二分法的SDP表述，以关于拷贝数线性的时间计算完美区分所需的量子态，并给出实现完美区分所需的最小信道拷贝数，进而肯定性地解决了Duan等人提出的猜想1。所有结果基于数值域的性质，其核心是张量积矩阵子空间数值域的最小角等于各子空间数值域最小角之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道区分在量子信息理论中至关重要，其中可完美区分的情形尤为受关注。现有工作缺乏对并行方案下完美区分所需信道拷贝数的高效计算方法，且Duan等人在IEEE ISIT 2016提出的关于并行使用次数刻画算子子空间族的猜想1尚未被解决。

Method: 针对并行方案下的完美量子信道区分任务，作者构建了一个半正定规划（SDP）表述，并结合二分法搜索，计算用于完美区分的量子态；该方法关于拷贝数具有线性时间复杂度。同时借助数值域（numerical range）的概念与基本性质进行分析，核心证明并使用了如下事实：矩阵子空间张量积的数值域的最小角，等于各个子空间数值域最小角之和。

Result: 所提出的SDP结合二分法能够以关于拷贝数线性的时间计算出实现完美区分的量子态，并确定了达到完美区分所需量子信道的最小拷贝数。据此，作者肯定性地解决了Duan、Guo、Li和Li（arXiv:1605.02294, IEEE ISIT 2016）的猜想1，该猜想刻画了完美区分一类特定算子子空间族所需的并行使用次数。

Conclusion: 在并行方案下，完美量子信道区分可通过SDP与二分法高效求解，所需信道最小拷贝数得以确定，Duan等人的猜想1被证明成立。数值域的最小角在张量积下具有可加性，是支撑上述结果的关键工具。

Abstract: Quantum channel discrimination plays a crucial role in quantum information theory. Of particular interest is the case in which the channels can be discriminated perfectly. In this work, we focus on the perfect quantum channel discrimination task in a parallel scheme. We develop an SDP formulation combined with a bisection procedure to compute a quantum state for perfect discrimination in time linear in the number of copies. In addition, we obtain the minimal number of copies of quantum channels to achieve perfect discrimination. Thanks to that, we settle in the affirmative Conjecture 1 of Duan, Guo, Li and Li [arXiv:1605.02294, IEEE ISIT 2016], which characterizes the number of parallel uses needed to discriminate perfectly a distinguished family of operator subspaces. All our results are obtained using the notion and basic properties of the numerical range. In particular, the key fact that we prove and use is that the minimal angle of the numerical range of a tensor product of matrix subspaces equals the sum of the minimal angles of the numerical ranges of the individual subspaces.

</details>


### [96] [Locally optimized variational evolution for quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20802)
*Carolin Wille,Max Marvell,Lauren Stewart,Max Murphy,Vinul Wimalaweera,Lesley Gover,Andrew G. Green*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于局部约化密度矩阵的变分时间演化原理，摆脱全局波函数的指数复杂度，利用热化导致的局部简化，给出闭式运动方程，并在量子硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多体动力学量子优势依赖全局波函数的指数复杂度，但局部可观测量并不继承这种复杂度，而由与系统总尺寸无关的局域复杂度主导。在热化系统中，局部可观测量丢失微观细节并弛豫到仅由少数参数决定的平衡值，这一区分未被现有方法利用。

Method: 提出变分时间演化原理，用局部约化密度矩阵上的代价函数替代全局态保真度；具体算法基于局部优化矩阵乘积态，给出类似时间依赖变分原理（TDVP）的闭式运动方程；并构造量子-经典对应版本，用量子器件评估局部代价，配合对采样噪声与硬件噪声鲁棒的优化策略。

Result: 该演化保留短时的相干动力学，同时在后期利用热化带来的简化；在Quantinuum H2与IBM Heron处理器上的原理验证实现成功复现了特征局域动力学。

Conclusion: 以局部复杂度为核心的变分时间演化原理可在保持短时相干性的同时利用热化简化，兼具经典与量子-经典实现形式，并在现有量子硬件上得到验证，为多体动力学模拟提供新路径。

Abstract: Conventional quantum advantage in many-body dynamics is based on avoiding the simulation cost on a classical computer that arises from the extensive exponential complexity of the global wavefunction. Local observables, however, do not inherit this extensive complexity and may instead be governed by an intrinsic local complexity that is independent of the total system size. This distinction is particularly relevant in thermalising systems, where local observables lose memory of microscopic details and relax towards equilibrium values determined by only a few parameters. Here we introduce a variational time-evolution principle that exploits this distinction by replacing global-state fidelity with a cost function defined on local reduced density matrices. The resulting evolution retains coherent short-time dynamics while exploiting the simplification produced by thermalisation at later times. The concrete algorithm we propose is based on locally optimising matrix-product states and admits closed-form equations of motion analogous to the time-dependent variational principle. We show that the same variational principle has a quantum-classical counterpart, combining quantum evaluation of the local cost with an optimisation strategy robust to both shot and hardware noise. Proof-of-principle implementations on Quantinuum H2 and IBM Heron processors recover the characteristic local dynamics.

</details>


### [97] [Environment Alignment and Redundant Record Formation in Imperfect-CNOT Quantum Darwinism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20823)
*Aleksander Lasek,Paweł Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究imperfect-CNOT模型中制备、相互作用角度与场无序如何共同影响量子达尔文主义下的冗余记录形成，揭示记录能力的非单调响应源于条件分支分离的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子达尔文主义通过环境冗余记录解释客观信息的涌现。已有工作表明不完美记录可被放大，且环境自演化能增强或抑制记录形成，但制备、相互作用角度与场无序三者的联合作用机制尚不清楚，需要建立更完整的几何图像。

Method: 采用带随机高斯耦合、纯态且无相互作用的环境量子比特的imperfect-CNOT模型；引入单一对齐参数Λ刻画制备与相互作用角度依赖的条件态可区分性；对比均匀局域场与等均方根强度的高斯分布场；推导碎片信息的精确表达式，并用最多24个环境量子比特进行数值模拟。

Result: 无场时Λ单独决定条件态可区分性的制备与角度依赖；增大相互作用不完美度或局域场强度既可改善也可抑制记录；该非单调响应由条件分支分离的几何解释；对纯初态环境，Z基对Holevo信息仍最优，故场辅助记录无需改变被记录的系统可观测量；无序会同时拓宽有益与有害的场效应；高平均信息与可靠碎片记录存在区别，且受碎片大小与有限观测窗口影响。

Conclusion: 局部信息获取的几何结构与实现稳健记录所需的碎片尺寸直接关联；分支可区分性分析仅依赖各环境量子比特的条件演化，可推广至保持系统指针基的其他相互作用。

Abstract: Quantum Darwinism explains objective information through redundant environmental records. Earlier work established that imperfect records can be amplified and that environment self-evolution can enhance or suppress their formation. We investigate how preparation, interaction angle, and field disorder combine in an imperfect-CNOT model with random Gaussian couplings and pure, noninteracting environment qubits. We find that, without fields, a single alignment parameter $Λ$ determines the preparation and interaction-angle dependence of conditional-state distinguishability. Increasing interaction imperfection or local field strength can improve or suppress recording. We explain this nonmonotonic response through the geometry of conditional branch separation. For the pure initial environments considered here, the $Z$ basis remains optimal for Holevo information, so field-assisted recording requires no change of the recorded system observable. Comparing uniform local field strengths with Gaussian-distributed strengths at equal root-mean-square strength shows how disorder broadens both beneficial and detrimental field effects. Exact expressions for fragment information and numerical simulations with up to 24 environment qubits quantify the distinction between high mean information and reliable records across fragments and throughout a finite observation window. These results connect the geometry of local information acquisition to the fragment sizes needed for robust recording. The branch-distinguishability analysis itself requires only the conditional evolution of each environment qubit and extends to other interactions that preserve a system pointer basis.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Time-resolved topology of aperiodically driven systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19348)
*Justin T. Cole,Christina Jörg,Terry A. Loring,Alexander Cerjan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出"演化历史谱局域化"框架，仅依赖有限时间幺正演化算子即可直接刻画陈拓扑，突破传统Floquet哈密顿量需周期性驱动的限制，并给出时间分辨的局域陈标记与局域能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动系统的拓扑相通常通过Floquet哈密顿量分类，其定义依赖重复的驱动周期。然而当系统演化是非周期或非线性时，该框架失效，即使鲁棒拓扑现象仍然存在。因此需要一种不依赖理想化Floquet周期的拓扑刻画方法。

Method: 引入演化历史谱局域化（evolution-history spectral localizer）框架，直接从系统的有限时间幺正演化算子出发分类陈拓扑；由此产生时间分辨的局域陈标记及其对应的局域能隙，用于在实际驱动动力学过程中追踪拓扑及其保护。并在准周期驱动与非线性光子模型中验证该方法。

Result: 在准周期驱动和非线性光子模型中，该方法成功识别出瞬态拓扑保护、其随后的丧失，以及非线性激发耗散后拓扑的恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析那些拓扑行为取决于有限时间动力学而非理想化Floquet周期的驱动光子系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: Topological phases of periodically driven systems are commonly classified through Floquet Hamiltonians, whose definition relies on a repeated driving cycle. However, this framework breaks down for systems whose realized evolution is aperiodic or nonlinear, even when robust topological phenomena appear to persist. Here, we introduce an evolution-history spectral localizer framework that classifies Chern topology directly from a system's finite-time unitary evolution operator. The method produces a time-resolved local Chern marker and an associated local gap, allowing topology and its protection to be tracked during the actual dynamics of a driven system. We demonstrate this approach in quasiperiodically driven and nonlinear photonic models, where it identifies transient topological protection, its subsequent loss, and the recovery of topology after nonlinear excitations disperse. The developed framework provides a route for analyzing driven photonic systems whose topological behavior depends on finite-time dynamics rather than an idealized Floquet cycle.

</details>


### [99] [Automatic Optical Alignment Using Projective Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19565)
*Bowen Li,Lukas Palm,Xin Wei,Marius Jürgensen,Zeyang Li,Yiming Cady Feng,Abhishek V. Karve,Jon Simon*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种软硬件自动对准框架，用低成本可加装电动镜架，结合投影几何前向模型与光电二极管优化器，实现复杂光路的高精度、快速自动对准，并在回射晶格原子传输系统上验证优于手动对准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂光路的对准是实验科学中的核心难题：该任务维度高、控制量之间强耦合，且许多系统物理可及性受限，人工对准耗时且精度受限，因此需要可自动化、低成本、易加装的解决方案。

Method: 构建硬件-软件框架：采用低成本、可加装的电动镜架；建立仅以物理镜面角度为自由变量的近轴紧凑前向模型，可毫秒级数值反解出目标光束轨迹所需的镜面角度；再以光电二极管信号驱动优化器，对几何解进行精细微调；收敛后的镜面设置被制成表格，实现毫秒级检索与秒级驱动。

Result: 在回射晶格原子传输系统上实验验证，对准的速度与精度均优于手动对准；收敛设置可快速检索并执行。

Conclusion: 该框架降低了复杂光路对准的人工投入，使物理可及性受限的实验具备可编程光学控制能力，并提升复杂光学架构的可扩展性。

Abstract: Aligning and maintaining complex optical beam paths is a central challenge across experimental science, because it is a high-dimensional task with strong cross-coupling between controls, often in systems with limited physical access. We present an automated hardware-software framework that resolves this alignment challenge using low-cost, retro-fittable motorized mounts driven by projective-geometry models and a photodiode-fed optimizer. A compact forward model describes the beam path to paraxial order with only the physical mirror angles left free, so it can be rapidly ($\sim$ms) numerically inverted to return the required mirror angles for a desired beam trajectory. A photodiode-fed optimizer then fine-tunes this geometric starting point, and converged mirror settings are tabulated for retrieval in milliseconds and actuation in seconds. We experimentally demonstrate the performance of this approach on a retro-reflected lattice atom-transport system, yielding improvements in both speed and precision over manual alignment. This framework reduces the manual effort required to align complex beam paths, enables programmable optical control in experiments with limited physical access, and enhances the scalability of complex optical architectures.

</details>


### [100] [Limits on the free-space group velocity of optical wave packets incorporating angular dispersion. Part~I, conventional angular dispersion: tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19576)
*Layton A. Hall,Ayman F. Abouraddy*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本教程论文（第一部分）表明，通过时空结构化的角散波包，原则上可在自由空间获得任意群速度，并给出统一的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为自由空间中空间结构化光场会因倾斜波矢增大平均群延迟而把群速度降到低于光速c；作者要澄清并纠正这一直觉，系统研究角散（AD）波包的群速度规律。

Method: 聚焦于具有角散的脉冲（传播角随波长变化），构造统一的理论表述，分析其群速度在横向场分布和传播轴上的恒定性，并考察偏离c所需的非傍轴大数值孔径条件及AD诱导的群速度色散。

Result: AD波包的群速度在横向和轴向均为常数，因而群延迟定义明确；原则上可实现自由空间中任意群速度（可不同于c）；但显著偏离c必然要求深处非傍轴区的大数值孔径，并伴随AD诱导的群速度色散；统一框架涵盖并联系了大量既有结果。

Conclusion: 自由空间中时空结构化可突破“结构化必然降低群速度”的直觉限制；常规可微角散存在数值孔径与色散代价，第二部分将介绍“不可微角散”以规避这些限制。

Abstract: A plane-wave optical pulse travels in free space at a group velocity $c$ (the speed of light in vacuum) measured along its propagation axis. It is sometimes thought that spatially structuring the field in free space reduces the group velocity below $c$ because -- intuitively -- the oblique wave vectors undergirding the wave packet increase the average group delay. Here we show that spatiotemporally structuring a pulsed optical beam (or wave packet) can yield -- in principle -- arbitrary group velocities in free space in contradistinction to this commonly held intuition. We devote our attention here to pulses endowed with angular dispersion (AD) where the propagation angle is wavelength dependent. This class of wave packets is particularly pertinent because their group velocity is constant over the transverse field profile and along the propagation axis, thereby yielding an unambiguous group delay. However, significant deviation of the AD-induced group velocity in free space from~$c$ inevitably requires a large numerical aperture that lies deep in the non-paraxial regime, and such wave packets experience AD-induced group-velocity dispersion. The formulation presented here captures a broad range of results, connecting them in a single framework. In Part~II of this tutorial, we describe recently identified `non-differentiable AD' (associated with propagation-invariant space-time wave packets) that helps circumvent the limits associated with conventional (differentiable) AD as outlined here.

</details>


### [101] [Limits on the free-space group velocity of optical wave packets incorporating angular dispersion. Part~II, non-differentiable angular dispersion: tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19580)
*Layton A. Hall,Ayman F. Abouraddy*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本教程第二部分提出"不可微角散(AD)"新概念，突破传统可微AD在非傍轴区才能大幅偏离光速的限制，在傍轴区即可无群速度色散地调控群速度，并统一了传播不变时空波包的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一部分表明，向准直光脉冲引入角散(AD)可调控自由空间波包群速度，且理论上群速度可取超光速、亚光速甚至负值；但群速度显著偏离c必须在非傍轴区以很大角度传播。本文旨在寻找突破这一限制的新机制。

Method: 提出并定义"不可微角散(non-differentiable AD)"：该AD剖面连续、光滑、处处可微，仅在某一波长处导数不存在（注意：并非间断、折点或奇点）。通过理论推导分析该不可微性对脉冲光束群速度调控的影响，并将其与第一部分传统(可微)AD的结论相联系，同时考察其与传播不变时空波包的关系。

Result: 利用不可微AD，在傍轴区即可实现群速度显著偏离c，且不产生群速度色散及任何高阶色散效应。此外，不可微AD是传播不变时空波包独特特性的基础，从而将大量结果统一于单一概念框架中。

Conclusion: 不可微AD是一种全新的角散形式，克服了传统可微AD在群速度调控上的傍轴限制，并可作为统一框架解释传播不变时空波包的独特性质。

Abstract: In Part~I of this tutorial, we showed that introducing angular dispersion (AD) into a collimated optical pulse allows for tuning the wave packet group velocity in free space. In principle, the group velocity can take on arbitrary values, whether superluminal, subluminal, or even negative. However, any significant deviation of the group velocity from $c$ (the speed of light in vacuum) necessitates propagation at large angles with respect to the optical axis in the non-paraxial regime. In Part~II of this tutorial, we describe the recent discovery of a new form of AD we refer to as `non-differentiable AD', which circumvents the limits imposed by conventional (differentiable) AD. Non-differentiable AD does \textit{not} refer to an AD profile that is discontinuous, contains a kink, or features a singularity. Rather, a non-differentiable AD profile is continuous, smooth, and is differentiable everywhere except for one wavelength at which the derivative is not defined. We show that salutary features follow from this non-differentiability with respect to tuning the group velocity of a pulsed beam in free space; namely, significant deviations in the group velocity away from $c$ are achievable in the paraxial regime while remaining free of group-velocity dispersion and all higher-order dispersive effects. This tutorial presents an outline of these recent results for tuning the group velocity of a wave packet via non-differentiable AD, connecting them with the corresponding results associated with conventional AD -- as outlined in Part~I. Moreover, we show that non-differentiable AD undergirds the unique characteristics of propagation-invariant space-time wave packets, thus unifying a large swathe of results in a single conceptual framework.

</details>


### [102] [Voltage-Current-Emission Framework of Time-Resolved Electroluminescence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19723)
*Hui Bao,Longjia Wu,Yanan Guo,Jianchang Yan,Ying Wang,Haizheng Zhong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了电压-电流-发光（Voltage-Current-Emission）框架，将半导体器件等效电路模型与电致发光过程的载流子动力学相结合，实现对LED时间分辨电致发光的定量理论描述，并验证了其在不同材料与器件结构LED中的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分辨电致发光是LED器件研发中的核心方法，但长期缺乏定量理论描述，难以从发光曲线中精确解读电流与载流子动力学。

Method: 将半导体器件的等效电路模型与电致发光过程的载流子动力学相结合，构建以脉冲电压为输入、注入电流为中间量、电致发光为输出的电压-电流-发光框架。

Result: 该框架能定量解释不同LED器件的特征，在多种材料与器件结构的LED上验证了普适性；并可模拟时间分辨电致发光曲线，定量描述LED工作过程中的电流与载流子动力学。

Conclusion: 电压-电流-发光框架为面向AI技术的高级LED器件提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Time-resolved electroluminescence has been a central methodology in the development of LED devices. However, it still lacks quantitative theoretical descriptions of time-resolved electroluminescence. By combining the equivalent circuit models of semiconductor devices and the carrier dynamics of electroluminescence processes, we here developed a Voltage-Current-Emission framework to illustrate time-resolved electroluminescence from the pulse voltage as input, to injection current and finally generate electroluminescence as output. The features of different LED devices can be quantitatively interpreted, confirming the universal applicability across LEDs with different materials and device structures. Importantly, the curves of time-resolved electroluminescence can be simulated to quantitatively describe the dynamics of current and carriers of LED operation. In all, the Voltage-Current-Emission framework offers theoretical guidance for achieving advanced LED devices for AI technology.

</details>


### [103] [Rapidity-Coupled Spin Dynamics in Pulsed Laser Fields from Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19756)
*N. S. Akintsov,A. P. Nevecheria,S. N. Andreev,Qing-Hua Qin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文给出静电子在线偏振平面波脉冲中静止系自旋极化角的解析解，据此验证了光前参考积分器与物理信息神经网络求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEP稳定少周期脉冲使亚周期场结构成为激光驱动极化电子源的控制参数，但此类脉冲的自旋求解器很少与精确结果对比验证。

Method: 基于Volkov轨道，用纵坐标与横向快度（rapidities）推导初始静止电子在线偏振平面波脉冲中静止系极化角的解析表达式Σ=2arctan(a_x/2)+a_e a_x（a_e为电子反常磁矩），并用该解检验光前参考积分器和仅在光前方程上训练的物理信息神经网络。

Result: 净旋转对所有CEP均为零；峰值脉冲内角度随CEP变化，双周期脉冲为3.02度，八周期脉冲为0.22度。物理信息神经网络在自旋部分达到4×10^-5精度，并可外推至无闭式解的椭圆偏振情形。

Conclusion: 该解析解为强场中CEP分辨的自旋动力学提供了经过验证的工具。

Abstract: Carrier-envelope-phase (CEP) stabilized few-cycle pulses make the sub-cycle field structure a control parameter for laser-driven polarized electron sources, yet spin solvers for such pulses are rarely tested against exact results. We show that for an electron initially at rest in a linearly polarized plane-wave pulse the rest-frame polarization angle follows the instantaneous vector potential, $Σ= 2\arctan(a_x/2) + a_e a_x$ with $a_e$ the electron anomaly: the net rotation vanishes for every CEP, while the peak intra-pulse angle varies over the CEP by 3.02 degrees for a two-cycle and by 0.22 degrees for an eight-cycle pulse. The result follows from the Volkov orbit in longitudinal and transverse rapidities. We use it to validate a light-front reference integrator and a physics-informed neural network trained only on the light-front equations, which reaches $4\times10^{-5}$ in the spin sector and carries over to elliptical polarization, for which no closed-form solution is known. This gives validated tools for CEP-resolved spin dynamics in intense fields.

</details>


### [104] [Aberration sensing in fluorescence microscopy using quantum correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19806)
*Shay Elmalem,Shaurya Aarav,Allard K. F. Hübler,Hugo Defienne,Dan Oron*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用荧光团固有的光子反聚束所产生的量子关联，通过SPAD阵列在宽场下直接从荧光发射中提取局部PSF自相关，实现无引导星、无迭代优化的快速直接PSF测量，为深组织自适应光学提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽场荧光显微成像的穿透深度从根本上受组织诱导像差限制，校正像差需精确表征点扩散函数（PSF）。然而现有波前传感技术要么依赖通常不可获得的引导星，要么依赖计算量大的间接相位恢复方法，且在低对比度区域表现不佳，因此亟需一种直接、非侵入的PSF测量手段。

Method: 利用标准荧光团固有的光子反聚束效应，将其视为"缺失"光子对的来源；借助单光子雪崩二极管（SPAD）阵列成像器在宽视场下采集光子关联统计量，直接从荧光发射本身提取局部PSF的自相关，无需迭代或复杂数值优化。

Result: 实现了宽场直接PSF反演，测量时间在1秒以内，具备实时运行潜力；该方法绕过了传统强度测量的局限，在低对比度区域亦具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将量子光学概念迁移至常规荧光探针，该技术为生命科学中的深组织自适应光学提供了一条稳健、直接的实现路径。

Abstract: Widefield fluorescence microscopy is an essential biological imaging tool, yet its penetration depth is fundamentally restricted by tissue-induced aberrations. Correcting these requires precise characterization of the point spread function (PSF), but existing wavefront sensing techniques rely either on often-unavailable guide stars, or on computationally heavy indirect phase retrieval methods, that could struggle in low-contrast regions. Here, we show that the PSF can be mapped directly and non-invasively by exploiting quantum correlations between photons. Standard fluorophores inherently exhibit photon antibunching, effectively acting as sources of ``missing'' photon pairs. By capturing these correlation statistics across a wide field of view using a single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) array imager, we extract the local PSF autocorrelation directly from the fluorescence emission itself. This approach bypasses the limitations of classical intensity measurements, requiring no iterative or complex numerical optimization. We demonstrate widefield direct PSF retrieval in a second or less, paving the way for real-time operation. By translating quantum optical concepts to conventional fluorescent probes, this technique provides a robust, direct pathway to deep-tissue adaptive optics in life sciences.

</details>


### [105] [Mo2TiC2Tx MXene Saturable Absorption for Neural Networks on a Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19849)
*Shadad Watad,Aviad Katiyi,Bar Favelukis,Muhammad Sharif Uddin,Anupma Thakur,Maxim Sokol,Babak Anasori,Alina Karabchevsky*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文量化了双过渡金属MXene（Mo2TiC2Tx）的非线性光学响应，并将其集成为硅波导非线性激活元件，在MNIST分类任务中实现98.39%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MXenes因宽带光学响应和化学可调性在集成非线性光子学中备受关注，但研究主要集中于Ti基组分，双过渡金属MXenes的非线性潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用800 nm下的Z-scan技术测量两种不同厚度Mo2TiC2Tx薄膜的可饱和吸收，提取有效非线性吸收系数；并将超薄Mo2TiC2Tx层集成到硅脊波导上实现紧凑型非线性光学激活函数，应用于神经网络仿真器。

Result: 两种厚度薄膜均表现出强可饱和吸收，β_eff约为-2.69×10^3至-0.88×10^3 cm/GW，非线性响应随激发强度升高而下降；提取的非线性吸收比同等条件下Ti3C2Tx MXene高约一个数量级；神经网络仿真器在MNIST基准上达到98.39%分类准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作将MXene材料平台拓展至Ti基组分之外，确立双过渡金属MXenes作为高性能集成非线性光子学与神经形态计算技术的有前景候选材料。

Abstract: MXenes have attracted considerable interest for integrated nonlinear photonics owing to their broadband optical response and chemical tunability, yet investigations have focused predominantly on Ti-based compositions, leaving the nonlinear potential of double-transition-metal MXenes largely unexplored. Here we quantify the nonlinear optical response of the double-transition-metal MXene \ce{Mo2TiC2T_x} and demonstrate its functionality as a waveguide-integrated nonlinear activation element for neuromorphic photonics. Z-scan measurements at \SI{800}{\nano\metre} reveal strong saturable absorption in films of two different thicknesses, yielding effective nonlinear absorption coefficients ($β_\mathrm{eff}$) between approximately $-2.69\times10^{3}$ and $-0.88\times10^{3}$ \si{\centi\metre\per\giga\watt}, with the nonlinear response decreasing at higher excitation intensities. The extracted nonlinear absorption is approximately one order of magnitude larger than that reported for \ce{Ti3C2T_x} MXene under comparable conditions. We integrate an ultrathin \ce{Mo2TiC2T_x} MXene layer onto a silicon rib waveguide to realize a compact nonlinear optical activation function, which, when implemented in a neural-network emulator, achieves $98.39\%$ classification accuracy on the MNIST benchmark. This work expands the MXene material platform beyond Ti-based compositions and establishes double-transition-metal MXenes as promising candidates for high-performance integrated nonlinear photonic and neuromorphic computing technologies.

</details>


### [106] [Broadband transverse mode directional couplers using partial Euler bends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19933)
*Airin Antony,Daniel Peace,Jacquiline Romero*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于部分欧拉弯折的几何设计框架，实现硅基SOI平台上前两个TE模式的宽带功率耦合，含模式（解）复用器及非平衡耦合器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模分复用与高维量子信息处理需在宽带宽内以任意比例合束/分束高阶横向模式；部分欧拉弯折在多模波导中具有更低弯曲损耗和模间串扰，故借其构建宽带耦合设计框架。

Method: 基于部分欧拉弯折的几何框架，设计前两个TE模式的定向耦合器；采用本征模展开与有限差分时域（FDTD）混合仿真方法。

Result: 仿真定向耦合器1-dB带宽为101–134 nm；最佳器件模式（解）复用器实测1-dB带宽90 nm，1550 nm处转换效率94.0%，串扰低于−18 dB。

Conclusion: 验证了部分欧拉弯折用于宽带横向模式定向耦合器的有效性，并提供通用设计框架。

Abstract: Transverse modes beyond the fundamental mode are increasingly being explored to increase information capacity in both mode-division multiplexing and high-dimensional quantum information processing. The ability to combine and separate these modes at arbitrary ratios over a broad bandwidth is central to these applications. Motivated by the lower bending loss and intermodal crosstalk of partial Euler bends in multimode waveguides, we develop a geometric framework based on partial Euler bends for broadband power coupling between the first two transverse electric modes on a silicon-on-insulator platform. Our methodology uses a hybrid of eigenmode expansion and finite-difference time-domain methods. Our designs feature a mode (de)multiplexer, as well as unbalanced couplers (2/3 and 1/3 splitters). The simulated 1-dB bandwidths of our directional couplers range from $101$ nm to $134$ nm. The best-performing fabricated mode (de)multiplexer has a measured 1-dB bandwidth of $90$ nm, a conversion efficiency of $94.0\%$ and crosstalk below $-18$ dB at $1550$ nm. Our results validate the use of partial Euler bends for broadband transverse mode directional couplers and provide a general framework for their design.

</details>


### [107] [Power-Bandwidth Scaling of Resonantly Coupled Soliton Microcombs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19941)
*Xinrui Luo,Kaixuan Zhu,Yuanlei Wang,Yinke Cheng,Haoyang Luo,Junqi Wang,Yiwen Yang,Zhenyu Xie,Bei-Bei Li,Qihuang Gong,Qi-Fan Yang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文研究通过辅助微谐振腔共振泵浦产生孤子微梳的功率效率问题，发现孤子维持与形成所需功率之间存在竞争关系，进而导出最优腔间耦合及输入泵浦功率随梳带宽的2/3次幂缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤子微梳带宽展宽需不断提高泵浦功率；借助辅助微谐振腔共振泵浦虽可降低维持孤子所需功率，却同时提高孤子形成所需功率，二者矛盾亟待澄清与优化。

Method: 建立包含腔间耦合的共振泵浦模型，分析孤子维持功率与形成功率随带宽变化的相反趋势，求解最优腔间耦合条件。

Result: 该竞争关系导致存在最优腔间耦合；预测最小输入泵浦功率随梳带宽呈2/3次幂缩放，而直接泵浦为二次缩放；实验验证了两种相反的功率趋势。

Conclusion: 研究给出了高功率效率、超宽带孤子微梳的设计准则。

Abstract: A soliton microcomb requires increasing pump power as its optical bandwidth is broadened. Resonant pumping through an auxiliary microresonator can reduce the power required to sustain a soliton, but simultaneously increases the power required for soliton formation. We show that this competition leads to optimal inter-resonator coupling, and the predicted minimum input pump power scales as the two-thirds power of the comb bandwidth, in contrast to the quadratic scaling under direct pumping. Experiments support the opposing power trends, providing a design rule for power-efficient, ultra-broadband soliton microcombs.

</details>


### [108] [High-capacity computing with self-rectification nonlinear optical neural processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19981)
*Ruicheng Ma,Siyu Dong,Yuzhi Shi,Yuchen Zhu,Hong Luo,Qiang Fu,Hadi Amata,Wolfgang Heidrich,Xiong Dun,Hongfei Jiao,Hui Zhang,Qinghua Song,Zeyong Wei,Zhanshan Wang,Ali Momeni,Romain Fleury,Xinbin Cheng*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出光学非线性神经处理单元（ONNPU），通过自整流机制实现全光非线性激活，其架构完全模仿数字神经网络，可无缝集成现有深度学习生态，并在9类任务上验证了性能，包括ImageNet上Vision Transformer达82.4% top-1准确率及超越GPT-2的故事生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后摩尔时代，光计算有望带来前所未有的速度与能效优势；然而，如何使用全光方法实现高效、实用的非线性激活仍是难题，制约了实用化的光学机器智能。

Method: 提出ONNPU，利用自整流机制实现全光非线性激活；其结构完全模仿数字神经网络，从而能够无缝对接既有深度学习生态，并集成进大规模Transformer模型中进行评测。

Result: 在决策、回归、生成共9项任务上完成基准测试：MNIST达98.07%，Fashion-MNIST达93.54%；集成于2.01亿参数Vision Transformer，在完整ImageNet（1000类）上取得82.4% top-1准确率；集成于1.17亿参数decoder-only Transformer，实现短篇故事生成并优于GPT-2。

Conclusion: ONNPU通过解决更复杂多样的深度学习任务，为实用化光学机器智能铺平道路，释放了高性能光计算的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and neural networks have driven groundbreaking innovations across numerous disciplines. Optical computing offers the promise of unprecedented speed and energy efficiency in the post-Moore era; however, achieving efficient, practical nonlinear activation using all-optical approaches remains a challenge. Here, we present an optical nonlinear neural processing unit (ONNPU) that implements all-optical nonlinear activation through a self-rectification mechanism. The ONNPU architecture perfectly imitates the structure of digital neural networks, enabling seamless integration with the established deep learning ecosystem. We benchmark ONNPU across nine diverse tasks spanning decision, regression and generation, including accuracies of 98.07% on MNIST and 93.54% on Fashion-MNIST. When integrated into a 201-million-parameter Vision Transformer, ONNPU achieves 82.4% top-1 accuracy on full ImageNet classification (1,000 categories); when integrated into a 117-million-parameter decoder-only Transformer, ONNPU enables short-form story generation that outperforms GPT-2. By addressing more complex and diverse deep learning tasks, ONNPU paves the way toward practical optical machine intelligence, unleashing significant potential for high-performance optical computing.

</details>


### [109] [Transient High-Harmonic Generation from Solids: From Spectroscopy and Control to Programmable Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20085)
*Pieter J. van Essen,Torben Ham,Zhonghui Nie,Peter M. Kraus*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本章综述固态高次谐波产生（HHG）光谱学与控制的最新进展，聚焦半导体与关联材料。内容涵盖HHG理论基础、实验信息提取方法、其对多种微观性质（能带结构、跃迁偶极矩、Berry相位、晶体对称性、载流子布居、退相干、晶格动力学）的敏感性，以及利用光激发、多色驱动场、波形工程和材料性质瞬态调控实现对谐波发射的增强、抑制与整形。研究将固态HHG定位为兼具光谱学与可编程非线性光学平台双重角色的技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态HHG已成为探测凝聚态体系电子结构与超快动力学的强大手段，但谐波发射同时依赖众多耦合的材料与激发参数，难以单一对应某一微观量，这一特性既带来挑战也提供了丰富的调控可能，因此有必要系统梳理其光谱学与控制方面的进展。

Method: 综述性方法：阐述HHG理论基础，总结从谐波发射中提取信息的实验途径，并归纳用于增强、抑制和整形谐波发射的调控手段，包括光激发、多色驱动场、波形工程以及材料性质的瞬态调控。

Result: HHG对能带结构、跃迁偶极矩、Berry相位、晶体对称性、载流子布居、退相干过程及晶格动力学等广泛微观性质表现出敏感性，可在飞秒至阿秒时间尺度上获取电子与结构动力学信息；同时，多参数耦合的强依赖性为谐波调控提供了多种途径，可实现谐波的增强、抑制与整形。

Conclusion: 固态HHG不仅是一种光谱学技术，更是一个可编程非线性光学平台，其应用涵盖紧凑型极紫外光源、超分辨显微成像及超快光子器件。

Abstract: High-harmonic generation (HHG) from solids has emerged as a powerful probe of electronic structure and ultrafast dynamics in condensed matter systems. In this chapter, we review recent advances in solid-state HHG spectroscopy and control, with a focus on semiconductors and correlated materials. We discuss the theoretical foundations of HHG and summarize experimental approaches for extracting information from harmonic emission. HHG has been shown to be sensitive to a wide range of microscopic properties, including band structure, transition dipole moments, Berry phases, crystal symmetry, carrier populations, dephasing processes, and lattice dynamics. This sensitivity enables access to electronic and structural dynamics on femtosecond and attosecond timescales and has established HHG as a versatile spectroscopic tool. At the same time, the strong dependence of HHG on many coupled material and excitation parameters makes the emitted harmonics intrinsically difficult to associate with a single microscopic quantity. Rather than representing a limitation alone, this sensitivity also provides numerous pathways for control. We review how photoexcitation, multicolor driving fields, waveform engineering, and transient modification of material properties can be used to enhance, suppress, and shape harmonic emission. These developments position solid-state HHG not only as a spectroscopic technique, but also as a platform for programmable nonlinear optics with applications ranging from compact extreme-ultraviolet sources to super-resolution microscopy and ultrafast photonic devices.

</details>


### [110] [Frequency Attraction and Repulsion in Lasers with Complex Mutual Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20132)
*Amit Pando,Tomer Hacohen,Eran Bernstein,Victor Shelukin,Asher Friesem,Nir Davidson*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究利用可重构数字简并腔激光器实验探究了含耗散与色散分量的复杂互耦合下的激光同步，发现复杂耦合可同时诱导频率吸引与排斥，并利用该频移显著增强多激光阵列的全局同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见耦合振荡器模型通常只考虑简单耦合，难以描述同时含耗散与色散分量的复杂互耦合下的激光同步行为，亟需实验平台加以验证与拓展。

Method: 采用可重构数字简并腔激光器，实现对系统参数的精确控制，研究复杂互耦合下激光的频率吸引与排斥现象，并与数值及理论结果进行对比。

Result: 复杂耦合能够同时引起频率吸引与频率排斥，这与常见耦合振荡器模型不同；这些频移可用于显著增强多激光阵列的全局同步，且复杂耦合激光的振幅动力学在其中起重要作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为探索非厄米物理及定制激光网络中的拓扑态提供了通用实验平台。

Abstract: We experimentally investigate laser synchronization under complex mutual coupling, featuring both dissipative and dispersive components. Using a reconfigurable digital degenerate cavity laser with precise control over system parameters, we show that complex coupling can induce both frequency attraction and repulsion, in contrast to common models of coupled oscillators. Furthermore, we show that these frequency shifts can be exploited to significantly enhance global synchronization in multi-laser arrays. Comparison with numerical and theoretical results indicate that amplitude dynamics of complex coupled lasers play an important role in the observed phenomena. Our results establish a versatile platform for exploring non-Hermitian physics and topological states in tailored laser networks.

</details>


### [111] [Characterization of spatially inhomogeneous chirp in ultrashort multielectron beams via femtosecond hole burning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20141)
*Yuichi Tachibana,Yuya Morimoto*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文结合PINEM谱烧孔与角度分辨能量分析，表征多电子超短脉冲中空间非均匀的时间-能量结构，并在37 keV、每脉冲最多17电子的条件下测得能量展宽与啁啾率随束角变化分别达100%和40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲电子束的时间-能量结构直接影响束驱动光产生与时间分辨显微成像，但多电子脉冲存在显著空间电荷效应，其时间-能量结构可能空间非均匀，传统整体表征无法捕捉这一特性。

Method: 将源于光子诱导近场电子显微镜（PINEM）效应的谱烧孔与角度分辨能量分析相结合，在每个束角上观测时间-能量关联；应用于37 keV、每脉冲最多17电子的脉冲。

Result: 测得能量展宽与啁啾率在整个束截面上分别变化达100%和40%，证实多电子脉冲的时间-能量结构具有显著空间非均匀性。

Conclusion: 多电子脉冲需以空间或角度分辨方式表征，空间调制光可用于高流通量脉冲电子束的整形。

Abstract: Direct observation of the temporal-energy structure of pulsed electron beams is crucial for beam-driven light generation and time-resolved microscopic imaging. In this study, we characterized the spatially non-uniform time-energy structure in multi-electron ultrashort pulses exhibiting significant space-charge effects. By combining spectral hole burning originating from the photon-induced near-field electron microscopy (PINEM) effect with angle-resolved energy analysis, we observed time-energy correlations at each beam angle. Applying this method to 37-keV pulses containing up to 17 electrons per pulse, we determined energy spread and chirp rate, which vary by up to 100% and 40%, respectively, across the entire beam. These findings highlight the importance of characterizing multi-electron pulses with spatial or angular resolution and suggest the utility of spatially modulated light for the shaping of high-flux pulsed electron beams.

</details>


### [112] [Path-Integrated Polarization Rotation Signatures in Subsea Networks: Cable Geometry Effects in 2023 Turkey Earthquakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20527)
*Mohammad M. Hosseini,Miquel Masanas,Giuseppe Parisi,Wesley Braga Melo,Alberto Marullo,Danilo Decaroli,Sergei K. Turitsyn,Antonio Napoli*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于2023年土耳其地震期间MedNautilus商用相干光模块的偏振数据流，揭示地震特征与光缆几何结构对偏振响应的共同主导作用，为光纤地震传感系统设计提供初步解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用既有商用相干光通信链路实现地震感知的可行性，并理解地震事件与光缆空间几何如何共同影响光偏振响应，从而为光纤地震传感系统的设计提供依据。

Method: 采集2023年土耳其地震期间MedNautilus链路中商用相干转发器输出的偏振数据流，结合地震特征与光缆几何结构进行关联分析。

Result: 发现光偏振响应同时受地震特征与光缆几何结构的基本支配，二者共同决定偏振变化模式。

Conclusion: 该研究为实现基于商用相干光模块的详细地震信号解读、并指导光纤地震传感系统设计迈出了第一步。

Abstract: We analyze polarization data streamed from a commercial coherent transponder, used in MedNautilus link, during 2023 Turkey earthquakes. We show how earthquake features and cable geometry fundamentally govern the optical polarization response, taking the first steps for detailed interpretation in designing fiber-based seismic sensing systems.

</details>


### [113] [High-order correlations and ultrafast Wigner negativities in bright-squeezed-vacuum-driven high-harmonic generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20559)
*Sebastián de-la-Peña,Heiko Appel,Marcelo F. Ciappina,Ofer Neufeld,Angel Rubio*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在收敛的多模希尔伯特空间中求解被明亮压缩真空驱动的二能级系统的全量子电动力学动力学，全面量化驱动场与发射谐波，揭示压缩真空驱动下谐波发射具有与相干激发定性不同的二、三阶光子关联，并产生阿秒尺度演化的显著Wigner函数负值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将高次谐波产生（HHG）拓展到量子光学区，可用非经典光态探测和调控强非线性光-物质相互作用；但该区制需对关联的电子-光子动力学做全量子处理，对宽带、强压缩场而言计算极具挑战，现有半经典采样或微扰方法难以直接获取多模量子态及其高阶关联。

Method: 构建全量子框架：在收敛的多模希尔伯特空间中求解被明亮压缩真空驱动的二能级系统的量子电动力学动力学；驱动场与发射谐波均被完全量子化，光-物质相互作用非微扰处理，从而直接获取多模量子态及其高阶关联。

Result: 压缩真空驱动产生的谐波发射，其二阶和三阶光子关联与相干激发情形存在定性差异；同时被驱动发射体的反作用强烈重塑入射压缩场，产生显著的Wigner函数负值，并在阿秒时间尺度上演化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了面向压缩光宽带强场动力学的全量子框架，为预测和解释量子HHG实验及其向更复杂发射体的拓展提供了途径。

Abstract: High-harmonic generation (HHG) is a prototypical strong-field process in which intense light drives matter to emit radiation at integer multiples of the driving frequency. Extending HHG into the quantum-optical regime offers new opportunities to probe and control strongly nonlinear light-matter interactions using nonclassical states of light. Yet describing this regime requires a fully quantum treatment of the correlated electron-photon dynamics, which becomes computationally challenging for broadband, strongly squeezed fields. Here we solve the quantum-electrodynamical dynamics of a two-level system driven by bright squeezed vacuum in a converged multimode Hilbert space. Both the driving field and emitted harmonics are fully quantized, with the light-matter interaction treated nonperturbatively. This enables direct access to the multimode quantum state and its higher-order correlations beyond semiclassical sampling or perturbative descriptions. We show that squeezed-vacuum driving produces harmonic emission with qualitatively distinct second- and third-order photon correlations compared with coherent excitation. Moreover, back-action from the driven emitter strongly reshapes the incident squeezed field, generating pronounced Wigner-function negativities that evolve on attosecond timescales. Our results establish a fully quantum framework for broadband strong-field dynamics with squeezed light and provide a route to predicting and interpreting quantum-HHG experiments and their extension to more complex emitters.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Battery Enhancement via Degenerate Optical Parametric Amplifier and Common Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20613)
*Y. Y. Yang,H. N. Liu,Gangcheng Wang,T. Z. Luan,H. Z. Shen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一种非互易量子电池，其中充电器与电池共享一个或两个公共库，充电器由简并光学参量放大器（DOPA）驱动。研究证明了在稳定的阈下区域DOPA可增强电池能量与平均充电功率，并通过耗散耦合不对称性优化进一步提升储能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现开放量子系统中可调控且高效的量子电池充电，需要利用耗散相互作用实现定向能量传输，同时引入额外增益机制来增强储能与充电功率。

Method: 构建非互易量子电池模型，充电器与电池共享一个或两个公共库，充电器由简并光学参量放大器（DOPA）驱动。通过库介导的耗散相互作用实现定向能量转移，并分析DOPA的非线性增益与泵浦相位对充电动力学的调控作用，进一步优化耗散耦合的不对称性。

Result: 在稳定的阈下区域，DOPA可增强电池能量与平均充电功率；非线性增益和泵浦相位可有效控制充电动力学；优化耗散耦合不对称性可进一步提高储能。这些特性在单公共库与双公共库两种构型中均成立。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种利用DOPA与耗散耦合不对称性协同调控的可调策略，可用于增强开放系统量子电池的充电性能，且适用于单公共库与双公共库两种配置。

Abstract: We investigate a nonreciprocal quantum battery in which the charger and battery share one or two common reservoirs, with the charger driven by a degenerate optical parametric amplifier (DOPA). Reservoir-mediated dissipative interactions enable directional energy transfer, while the DOPA provides additional parametric am plification. We show that, in the stable below-threshold regime, the DOPA can enhance the battery energy and average charging power, with the nonlinear gain and pump phase providing effective control of the charging dynamics. Further optimization of the dissipative-coupling asymmetry improves the stored energy. These fea tures persist for both single- and two-common-reservoir configurations, demonstrating a tunable strategy for enhancing open-system quantum-battery charging.

</details>


### [115] [A Simplified Model for Linear Mode Coupling in Multimode Fibers with Experimental Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20617)
*Paolo Carniello,Filipe M. Ferreira,Fabio A. Barbosa,Ming-Jun Li,Norbert Hanik*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种用于渐变折射率多模光纤中线性耦合的新型代理模型，计算效率高于现有模型，且可通过单一可测参数调节，并在90模式偏振光纤上进行了数值与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有渐变折射率多模光纤线性耦合模型计算效率不足，且缺乏便于标定的简易参数化方案，需要更高效、可调且易测量的建模方法。

Method: 构建一种代理模型（surrogate model）来描述渐变折射率多模光纤中的线性耦合，模型仅通过一个可测量的参数进行调参，并在90模式偏振光纤（90-polarization-mode fiber）上开展数值仿真与实验基准测试。

Result: 在90模式偏振光纤上完成数值与实验基准对比，验证了模型的准确性与计算效率优势。

Conclusion: 该代理模型在保持精度的同时提升了计算效率，并凭借单参数可调特性具备良好的实用性与可标定性。

Abstract: We propose a novel surrogate model for linear coupling in graded-index multimode fibers that is computationally more efficient than existing models, and can be tuned through a single measurable parameter. The model is benchmarked numerically and experimentally on a 90-polarization-mode fiber.

</details>


### [116] [Volumetric Evanescent Edge Coupling for Fiber-to-Chip Optical I/O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20686)
*Hamdy Elshehaby,Omar Bakheet,Mohamed A. Swillam,Mohamed Elkabbash*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出"体式边缘耦合"（volumetric edge coupling）方案，通过在芯片边缘构建三维结构，使光耦合发生在二维区域而非单条线上，以突破共封装光学中光纤-芯片接口对通道扩展的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共封装光学的光I/O扩展受制于光纤-芯片接口：传统边缘耦合损耗低、带宽大，但通道仅能沿芯片端面单排排列；表面耦合器则要么窄带，要么难以高良率制造，且与后道金属布线争夺空间。

Method: 研究基于KOH腐蚀硅腔54.7°侧壁、由全内反射（TIR）介导的倏逝耦合单元通道实现，耦合剖面由楔形埋氧脊（buried-oxide ridge）整形，并采用时域有限差分（FDTD）仿真进行验证。

Result: 仿真预测在1550 nm处峰值耦合效率为88%（-0.56 dB），1-dB带宽86.45 nm，覆盖C波段；垂直对准容差约±2 μm，对5 μm以内的横向偏移不敏感，角度1-dB容差为±0.8°；反射光场可提供潜在对准信号。

Conclusion: 体式边缘耦合为多波导与多芯光纤多核的对接提供了可行路径，同时保留从芯片周边的光接入，并有望降低未来多核实现中的主动对准开销。

Abstract: Scaling optical input/output for co-packaged optics is limited by the fiber-to-chip interface. Conventional edge coupling offers low loss and broad bandwidth but confines channels to a single row along the chip facet. On the other hand, surface couplers are either narrowband or difficult to fabricate with high yield and, importantly, compete with back-end metal routing. In this work, we introduce volumetric edge coupling, in which the chip edge is structured in three dimensions so that optical coupling can occur over a two-dimensional region rather than along a single line, providing a route toward interfacing multiple waveguides with multiple cores of a multicore fiber while preserving optical access from the chip perimeter. We investigate a single-channel realization based on total-internal-reflection (TIR)-mediated evanescent coupling through the 54.7$^\circ$ sidewall of a KOH-etched silicon cavity, with the coupling profile shaped by a wedged buried-oxide ridge. Finite-difference time-domain simulations predict a peak coupling efficiency of 88% (-0.56 dB) at 1550 nm and a 1-dB bandwidth of 86.45 nm spanning the C-band. The design further exhibits a vertical alignment tolerance of approximately $\pm$2 $μ$m, weak sensitivity to transverse offsets up to 5 $μ$m, and a $\pm$0.8$^\circ$ 1-dB angular tolerance. The reflected optical field also provides a potential alignment signal, offering a path toward reduced active alignment overhead in future multicore implementations.

</details>
